We demonstrate the advantage of the proposed PC layer over standard transformers in Llama-1B pre-t","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2606_06470.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LLM训练中权重矩阵的奇异值谱会随训练动态退化，导致信号传播条件变差（小奇异值坍塌使深层信号衰减），而主流归一化方法（RMSNorm、QK-Norm）作用于激活输出而非权重空间。现有权重空间约束方法（如谱归一化）需要显式矩阵分解，计算成本过高。论文寻求一种轻量级、与反向传播兼容的权重谱控制方法。","importance":"将多项式预条件器引入LLM权重参数化，提供了一种无需SVD即可控制权重奇异值谱的机制。训练中通过低次多项式重塑谱，推理时可合并回原始架构无额外开销。从预训练角度看，这是继RMSNorm、QK-Norm之后又一个潜在的架构-level稳定训练组件，且与最近的权重归一化/谱约束（Loshchilov等2025年的行列归一化、超球约束等）一脉相承。","technical_approach":"预条件（PC）层的核心思想是用矩阵多项式g(W)作用于权重矩阵W。由于多项式在矩阵的SVD分解上只改变奇异值（g(W)的奇异值为{|g(σ₁)|, …, |g(σᵣ)|}），因此可以通过选择合适的多项式g来显式地\"提升小奇异值、压缩大奇异值\"，从而收窄谱的分布范围。训练结束后，预条件化的权重可合并回原架构，不引入任何推理开销。关键的LLM特定修改包括：幅度调整操作（范数恢复和可学习缩放），以及选择在哪些层应用预条件化。","experiments":"在Llama-271M和Llama-1B模型上，配合AdamW和Muon两种优化器进行预训练实验。Llama-1B上的结果：使用AdamW时达到相同损失所需token数减少约2×，使用Muon时减少约1.13×；两种优化器下零样本下游准确率均有提升。同时验证了PC层确实改善了权重谱的条件数。理论部分证明：对深度线性网络，若所有权重的条件数有上界，则梯度下降以依赖条件数的速率几何收敛到全局极小值。","reading_guide":"优先阅读第3节（PC层的具体构造和多项式选择）和实验部分（第4节）。理论证明（第2-3节中关于深度线性网络的收敛分析）对理解设计动机有帮助但非必需。LLM特定修改（幅度恢复、可学习缩放）是实际工作中可复用的细节，需注意。预计精读需1-1.5小时，泛读摘要和实验结果约15分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"During LLM training, the singular value spectra of weight matrices can degrade dynamically, leading to poor signal propagation conditioning (collapsed small singular values cause signal attenuation in deep layers), while mainstream normalization methods (RMSNorm, QK-Norm) act on activation outputs rather than weight space. Existing weight-space constraint methods (e.g., spectral normalization) require explicit matrix factorization and are computationally expensive. The paper seeks a lightweight, backpropagation-compatible approach to weight spectrum control.","importance":"Introduces polynomial preconditioners into LLM weight parameterization, providing a mechanism to control weight singular value spectra without SVD. The spectrum is reshaped via low-degree polynomials during training, and the preconditioned weights can be merged back into the original architecture with no inference overhead. From a pretraining perspective, this represents another potential architecture-level training stability component following RMSNorm and QK-Norm, and is aligned with recent work on weight normalization/spectral constraints (Loshchilov et al. 2025 row/column normalization, hypersphere constraints, etc.).","technical_approach":"The core idea of the Preconditioning (PC) layer is to apply a matrix polynomial g(W) to the weight matrix W. Since the polynomial only modifies singular values on the SVD of the matrix (the singular values of g(W) are {|g(σ₁)|, …, |g(σᵣ)|}), an appropriate polynomial g can be chosen to explicitly 'boost small singular values and compress large ones', thereby narrowing the spectral range. After training, preconditioned weights can be merged back with no inference overhead. Critical LLM-specific modifications include magnitude adjustment operations (norm recovery and learnable scaling), and selective application to specific layers.","experiments":"Pretraining experiments conducted on Llama-271M and Llama-1B with both AdamW and Muon optimizers. On Llama-1B: reaching the same loss requires ~2× fewer tokens with AdamW and ~1.13× fewer tokens with Muon; zero-shot downstream accuracy improves under both optimizers. The paper also verifies that PC layers improve weight spectrum conditioning. compute 增长而下降，而且这种关系在不同模型规模上保持稳定。这里要重点看图 1 和方法部分对应的拟合式：如果图上只是局部单调，那说明规律较弱；如果在较宽 compute 区间内都能被简单函数拟合，才说明它有 recipe 价值。\n\n第二类是 equivalent pre-training compute 是否真能定位 checkpoint。论文的关键主张是，来自任意训练阶段的 diffusion latent 更贴近 LLM 主干的建模方案：连续思维不是外挂模块，而是被放进同一个 causal stream 里，用 tractable likelihood 统一训练和采样。第二，它把“中间推理变量”重新拉回到生成建模问题，而不只是推理技巧问题。对预训练研究员来说，真正有价值的是这个接口设计：如果未来希望在 pretrain 或 mid-train 阶段引入高带宽中间状态，又不想放弃 AR 生态里的 sampling、scoring、RL、serving 兼容性，这篇工作提供了一个清晰方向。量级上，它还不是成熟范式，实验主要集中在代码推理，规模也有限，但方法层面的完成度较高。","technical_approach":"$2a","experiments":"实验主要在代码推理与程序合成任务上，包括 MBPP、MBPP+、HumanEval、HumanEval+ 和 LiveCodeBench v6。作者比较了显式文本 CoT、已有 latent reasoning 基线，以及 NF-CoT。主结论有两点：一是 NF-CoT 在 pass@k 或 pass rate 上优于显式 CoT 和先前 latent baseline；二是在中间推理成本上明显更低，因为连续 thought 序列长度 K 通常远小于文本 CoT token 长度 L。\n\n全文里更值得注意的是几类分析实验。其一，作者研究了 latent thought length 与性能的关系，说明连续 thought 并不是越长越好，而是存在压缩率与可建模性的折中。其二，likelihood-based sampling 的实验表明，既然 thought path 有显式概率，便可以像文本 CoT 一样做基于概率的采样和 reranking，这一点是很多 latent CoT 方法做不到的。其三，policy-gradient refinement 显示在连续 thought 空间上继续优化是可行的，并且能带来额外收益，说明 tractable density 不是纸面优点，而是能转化成训练接口。\n\n摘要没有给出完整数字，全文的重点也不只是单个 benchmark 提升幅度，而是展示一套完整闭环：显式 CoT 监督压缩为连续 thought，thought 可精确建模，推理时可左到右采样，之后还能在同一空间上做 RL。若精读实验，建议特别留意与 explicit CoT 的 token 成本对比、与 diffusion latent 的采样效率对比，以及 flow 结构和 thought length 的消融。","reading_guide":"建议按“问题—接口—实现—实验”顺序读。先看 flow。若只记一个核心点，就是：encoder latent e 适合压缩语义，但不适合直接做因果生成；flow 映射后的 u 才是给 LLM 采样和打分用的 thought space。然后看联合目标函数，理解 continuous thought likelihood 和 answer LM likelihood 如何耦合。\n\n实验部分优先看主结果表、thought length 在更大模型上是否仍划算；三，这种 latent causal stream 能否在预训练阶段直接引入，而不是依赖显式 CoT 蒸馏。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a central tension in latent reasoning. Once intermediate reasoning is moved from textual CoT to continuous latent states, most methods lose the properties that make autoregressive language models practical and effective: left-to-right sampling, exact likelihood scoring, compatibility with standard causal decoding and KV-cache, and a unified generation process for both reasoning and final answers. Existing approaches usually give up at least one of these. NF-CoT asks whether continuous reasoning can be treated as a first-class causal sequence object, with the same sampling and scoring interface as tokens.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is larger than the benchmark setting suggests. The paper offers a latent-reasoning design that stays close to the native LM interface instead of bolting on a separate diffusion-style generator. More importantly, it reframes intermediate reasoning as a generative modeling problem with tractable density, not just a prompting trick. For pretraining researchers, the useful idea is the interface: if future models want higher-bandwidth internal computation without abandoning the autoregressive ecosystem—sampling, scoring, RL, and serving compatibility—this is a concrete path. the","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2606_06031.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决 masked diffusion language model 推理中的纠错耦合问题。PRISM 会用同一次前向传播给 token 打质量分，并为被重采样的位置产生替换 logits。问题是，那些已经被判为低质量的 token 仍然参与条件上下文，导致模型用受污染的上下文来修正错误。另一个问题是 deterministic top-K remasking 容易反复选择同一批低分位置，使纠错过程过于贪心，并可能带来多样性下降。","importance":"这篇对新型语言建模范式更直接相关。它没有提出新的 diffusion LM 训练目标，而是指出 masked diffusion LM 的生成质量很大程度受 decoding policy 影响。对预训练研究的启发是：非自回归或半并行语言模型不能只看训练 loss 和并行解码步数，推理时的状态更新规则会改变错误传播路径。NAVIRA 的贡献量级是推理策略层面的增量改进，但它抓住了 MDM 相对 AR 解码的关键短板：并行更新带来的局部依赖错误如何被发现和清除。","technical_approach":"NAVIRA 保持 pretrained MDM backbone 和 PRISM 的质量头训练目标不变，只改推理。第一步前向传播只用于估计每个已生成 token 的质量分，并从低质量位置中选择需要 remask 的 token。随后把这些位置重新置为 mask。第二步再做一次前向传播，用清理后的上下文计算替换 token 的 logits。这样，被判定为不可靠的 token 不会继续污染自己的再生成条件。第二个改动是 stochastic remasking：不是固定选择质量分最低的 top-K，而是根据质量分构造温度控制的采样分布。低温更偏向集中修正低分 token，高温提供更多探索；scheduled temperature 则试图在早期探索和后期收敛之间折中。代价是每次修正需要两次前向传播，因此论文用 matched forward-pass budget 来控制比较。","experiments":"实验集中在 unconditional text generation，并固定 backbone 与 PRISM 训练方式，只比较解码策略。论文比较了 deterministic 与 stochastic remasking、固定温度与温度调度，以及不依赖学习质量头的 uncertainty-based baseline。在相同前向传播预算下，decoupled remasking 改善 fluency，因为再生成时上下文更干净；stochastic remasking 在较大解码预算下缓解 entropy collapse，更好地保留多样性。LLM-based evaluation 显示 scheduled stochastic remasking 的主观生成质量更好。给定正文片段没有列出完整表格数字，因此这里不补充未提供的具体分数。","reading_guide":"泛读建议用 20 分钟。先看 Introduction 和 Preliminaries，弄清 PRISM 的单次前向耦合在哪里：quality scoring 和 replacement logits 来自同一个受污染状态。然后看 NAVIRA 的 two-pass decoding 公式或伪代码，重点关注 remask 后再前向的上下文变化。实验部分优先看 matched forward-pass budget 的设置，否则两次前向的收益容易和额外计算混在一起。消融里温度调度、entropy collapse 和 diversity 指标最值得看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a decoding failure mode in masked diffusion language models. In PRISM-style inference, the same forward pass scores token quality and produces logits for replacement tokens. Tokens judged unreliable still remain in the conditioning context for their own regeneration, so correction can be conditioned on corrupted context. A second issue is greedy deterministic top-K remasking, which can repeatedly revisit the same low-scoring positions and reduce diversity.","importance":"This is directly relevant to alternative language modeling paradigms. The paper does not introduce a new diffusion-LM training objective; its contribution is an inference-time policy for masked diffusion decoding. The main lesson for pretraining researchers is that non-autoregressive and parallel-update language models cannot be evaluated only through training loss or nominal decoding parallelism. The state-update rule at inference time determines how local dependency errors propagate or get removed.","technical_approach":"NAVIRA keeps the pretrained masked diffusion backbone and the PRISM quality-head objective fixed. It changes only decoding. The first forward pass estimates token-level quality and selects positions to remask. Those positions are then replaced by mask tokens. A second forward pass recomputes generation logits from this cleaned context, so tokens marked as unreliable no longer condition their own replacements. NAVIRA also replaces deterministic top-K remasking with temperature-controlled stochastic remasking. Lower temperatures focus correction on low-quality positions; higher temperatures allow more exploration. Scheduled temperatures are used to balance early exploration with later refinement. The cost is an extra forward pass, so the paper compares methods under matched forward-pass budgets.","experiments":"The experiments focus on unconditional text generation while holding the backbone and PRISM training setup fixed. The paper compares deterministic and stochastic remasking, fixed and scheduled temperatures, and uncertainty-based baselines that do not use a learned quality head. Under matched forward-pass budgets, decoupled remasking improves fluency by regenerating from a cleaner context. Stochastic remasking helps preserve diversity and reduces entropy collapse when larger decoding budgets are used. LLM-based evaluation further favors scheduled stochastic remasking for perceived generation quality. The provided text does not include the full numerical tables, so exact scores are not reconstructed here.","reading_guide":"A 20 minute skim is sufficient. In the experiments, check the matched forward-pass budget setup first; otherwise the benefit of two-pass decoding can be confused with extra compute. Unlike dense models, however, MoE models are sensitive to routing instability: small quantization-induced perturbations can change the top-$k$ expert selection, altering the computation path and degrading model quality. We propose Value-and-Structure Routing Alignment for","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05688.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 MoE 模型做后训练量化时，一个 dense 模型里不明显、但在 MoE 里很致命的问题：量化误差会改变 router 的 top-k 专家选择，导致 token 走到不同 expert，计算路径被改写，最终性能下降。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是核心推进，更偏部署侧，但对 MoE 基座研究仍有启发。启发点在于：MoE 的脆弱性不只来自数值重建误差，还来自离散路由边界的扰动。这个观察对训练期的 router 正则、负载均衡、margin 设计、以及后续做低比特 MoE serving 都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"作者提出 VSRAQ，属于可插拔的 MoE 专用 PTQ 校准目标，而不是一套独立量化算法。它在常规 reconstruction loss 之外，加了 router alignment loss，并把对齐拆成两部分。第一部分是 value alignment，去匹配量化前后的 router logits 或相关分数，且对路由敏感区域加权，让靠近决策边界的 token 受到更强约束。第二部分是 structure alignment，不只看数值接近，还显式保持被选中 expert 之间的相对排序，以及第 k 个被选 expert 与附近未选 expert 之间的 margin，避免 rank inversion 和 boundary violation。方法可以接到现有 PTQ 流程上，推理时没有额外开销。和已有 router-aware quantization 相比，它的区别在于不满足于“分数差不多”，而是把 top-k 决策的结构稳定性也纳入目标。","experiments":"从全文描述看，实验重点应是三类指标：量化后语言模型质量下降幅度、expert-selection consistency，以及不同 bit-width/不同基线 PTQ 框架下的稳健性。作者声称在近期 MoE foundation models 上，VSRAQ 能减少量化导致的路由变化，并降低性能退化。阅读时重点看：相对 GPTQ/AWQ 类基线，加入 VSRAQ 后 perplexity 或下游指标改善多少；路由一致率提升多少；value alignment 和 structure alignment 分别拿掉时退化多大。如果这些消融成立，说明“结构对齐”不是装饰项，而是 MoE 量化里真正缺失的目标。","reading_guide":"泛读即可。先看 Introduction，理解作者为什么认为 MoE 量化和 dense 量化不是同一个问题。然后看方法部分里 structure alignment 的定义，尤其是 expert 排序和 top-k 边界 margin 怎么写进 loss。最后看实验表里路由一致率和主任务指标的对应关系。预计 20–30 分钟能抓住主要内容。若只关心研究启发，不必深究量化实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a MoE-specific failure mode in post-training quantization: small quantization errors can perturb router scores enough to change top-k expert selection, so the token is sent through a different computation path and model quality drops.","importance":"This is not a core pre-training paper, but it is still relevant to MoE foundation-model research. The useful takeaway is that MoE sensitivity is not only about numerical reconstruction error. It is also about preserving discrete routing decisions and the margins around those decisions. That insight can inform router regularization, routing-margin design, and low-bit MoE deployment strategy.","technical_approach":"The method, VSRAQ, is a plug-in calibration objective for MoE post-training quantization rather than a standalone quantizer. It augments the standard reconstruction loss with a router-alignment term and splits that term into two components. Value alignment matches pre- and post-quantization router logits or routing-relevant scores, with extra emphasis on sensitive regions near routing boundaries. Structure alignment goes beyond score matching and explicitly preserves the relative ordering among selected experts as well as the margin between the k-th selected expert and nearby non-selected experts. The goal is to prevent rank inversions and boundary violations that would alter top-k routing. The method can be integrated into existing PTQ pipelines and adds no inference-time overhead. Relative to prior router-aware quantization work, the main difference is that it treats routing as a structured decision problem, not just a value-matching problem.","experiments":"The key results to look for are: reduced degradation in language-model quality after quantization, higher expert-selection consistency, and robustness across bit-widths and PTQ backbones. The paper claims improvements on recent MoE foundation models. The most informative numbers are the gains over standard PTQ baselines such as GPTQ/AWQ-style methods, the increase in routing consistency, and the ablations removing value alignment or structure alignment. If the structure term matters materially in those ablations, that supports the paper’s main claim that preserving routing geometry is necessary for MoE quantization.","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Read the introduction to understand why MoE quantization is qualitatively different from dense-model quantization. Then read the definition of structure alignment, especially how expert ranking and top-k boundary margins are encoded in the loss. Finally, inspect the tables linking routing consistency to end-task quality. deter","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05742.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在不引入草稿模型（model-free）的前提下，如何让 已生成历史）里找语义相关位置，解决“候选不可达”的硬失败。之后对候选再用 hidden-state 相似度 rerank，选与当前解码状态更一致的 anchor。\n2）自适应复用（从单 span 拷贝到小树）：若 anchor 来自 lexical match，先从 memory 拷贝一条主 continuation（保持高精度复用）；但不把它当唯一假设，而是在 anchor 处用 target 模型的 next-token 分布引入少量 branch tokens（覆盖多解/歧义）；再做一次 successor reuse：如果分支后的 token 在 memory 里也能对齐到后继位置，就再额外复用 1 个 token 扩展草稿。最终形成一个很小的 draft tree，用 tree attention 让 target 一次前向验证并尽可能多接受 token。\n核心取舍很明确：语义检索只在 no-hit 时触发，避免把高精度的 lexical reuse 变成“到处找相似句子”的噪声；分支规模受控，避免 tree verification 的开销吞掉收益。","experiments":"全文宣称在 input-guided generation、code editing、reasoning 等基准上都有稳定加速，最高速度提升 Existing systems use unified token-level KV management and dense layouts, which mismatch the per-head sparsity structure of real workloads. This mismatch leads to low reuse rate of position-independent KV cache, unaddressed FFN overhead that accounts for over half of short-context latency, and 4.9-7.6x kernel penalty from sparse attention masks that disable the FlashAttention fast path, resulting in far lower real-world gains than theoretical predictions.","importance":"The observed per-head sparsity patterns from real serving workloads provide empirical evidence for attention architecture design and redundant head pruning during pretraining. The co-design principle of aligning storage, computation, and workload granularity also offers clear direction for inference-friendly design in pretraining.","technical_approach":"RedKnot is a serving system built on SGLang with three co-designed mechanisms: 1) Head-class sparsification: offline classification of each (layer, head) pair into global heads (12-15%, re-prefilled on reuse) and local heads (85-88%, reused directly within a sliding window), with adaptive runtime promotion of local heads via edge-mass signal to avoid cascading staleness of token-level patches. 2) SegPagedAttention: replaces dense KV layout with per-(layer, head) paged KV store and fused varlen attention kernel, physically retaining only tokens required by each head to stay on the FlashAttention fast path and eliminate mask-induced kernel penalty. 3) Sparse FFN: computes FFN only for top-k tokens with highest attention scores to address the 57-62% FFN overhead in 2-8K short-context workloads that no attention-side optimization can cover. Gains from the three mechanisms are multiplicative.","experiments":"Evaluated on an 8x NVIDIA H800 server across Mistral-7B, Qwen3-32B, Llama-3.3-70B, 6 QA datasets, and context lengths from 8K to 128K. Compared to dense baselines, RedKnot delivers 1.6-3.54x time-to-first-token speedup, 4.7-7.8x higher concurrent sessions per GPU, 67-79.5% reduction in prefill FLOPs, while matching or exceeding end-to-end accuracy.","reading_guide":"For skimming, first read section 3 on the three structural mismatches of existing serving systems, then review the three core mechanisms in section 4, with focus on empirical statistics of per-head sparsity. targets the engineering gap between sparse-attention algorithms and real serving speedups. Modern LLM serving systems store KV caches in a paged, physically non-contiguous layout with indirect addressing. This layout breaks the assumptions of standard tensor frameworks and makes new sparse-attention variants hard to express, deploy, and evaluate. The issue is sharper for dynamic sparsity, where the sparsity pattern is computed online and can dominate the cost of the attention operator itself.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to long-context and long-generation model research. Reasoning, agentic workloads, and RL rollouts put heavy pressure on decode-time KV movement. A sparse-attention idea is only useful for model design if it can be evaluated under a realistic serving stack. The main lesson is that sparse attention should be treated as a system-level abstraction problem, not just a kernel optimization problem. Pattern construction, paged-tensor reductions, Top-K selection, and integration with prefix caching often decide whether the method works in practice.","technical_approach":"Vortex introduces vTensor, a page-centric tensor abstraction for computation over paged KV layouts. The frontend is a Python-embedded language for specifying how sparsity patterns are generated and how attention is computed. The backend integrates with modern LLM serving systems and executes operations efficiently on non-contiguous paged memory. The paper positions Vortex against systems such as FlashInfer and FlexAttention: those systems are effective once the sparsity pattern is known, but they do not fully address dynamic sparse attention where pattern generation is part of the algorithm. Vortex also differs from custom-kernel implementations for individual methods, because it tries to make paged GEMM, Reduce, Top-K, and attention compositionally programmable.","experiments":"The provided full-text excerpt contains limited experimental details. It also notes that the original Quest implementation is 44.4x slower than SGLang full attention in a real serving setup and takes nearly two hours to evaluate Llama 3.1-8B on AMC23. These numbers support the paper’s central claim that optimized sparse kernels alone do not guarantee end-to-end gains. The abstract states that Vortex translates theoretical efficiency into real throughput improvements, but the excerpt does not include the full latency, throughput, memory, or ablation results.","reading_guide":"Skim in 30 to 45 minutes. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05232.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是多模态基础模型推理阶段的视觉 token 压缩设计问题。现有方法通常把 pruning、merging、pooling、adaptive reweighting 当成彼此独立的技巧，再手工决定在哪些层压缩、保留多少 token、用哪一种算子。论文指出，真正困难的不只是设计某一个压缩算子，而是如何在统一框架里同时决定“何时压缩、压到什么程度、采用哪种信息转移方式”，并在精度与算力约束下自动搜索出更合适的组合。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接相关性中等，核心价值不在预训练 recipe，而在统一建模和可微搜索视角。它给出的启发是：许多看似分散的 token reduction 技术，其实可以被放进一个连续算子空间里，再通过可微优化而不是人工经验去选结构。这种思路对多模态 LLM 的训练后加速、长上下文视觉 token 管理、甚至训练期的动态计算分配都有参考意义。贡献量级更像是“把已有压缩操作抽象成统一参数化空间，并证明搜索优于手工 recipe”，不是新的基础建模范式。","technical_approach":"方法的核心是 Efficient Operator Search（EOS）。它先假设一个冻结的多模态基础模型，在若干 decoder layer 上对视觉 token 做压缩，但不再预先固定压缩策略，而是把三类决策都参数化为可学习变量。\n\n第一部分是统一算子空间。每个激活的压缩层先根据 token importance 选出 K 个 anchor token，其余 token 作为待压缩集合。随后计算待压缩 token 与 anchor 的归一化相似度矩阵，并通过温度参数 τ 控制 assignment 的尖锐程度：τ 小时更接近 hard merge，τ 大时更接近 soft redistribution 或 pooling。再通过 transfer gate 控制被压缩 token 的信息是否保留并转移到 anchor 上；如果 gate 关闭，就接近 pruning；如果开启，再结合 assignment 形式，就能落在 merge、pool、reweight 等不同 regime。论文的关键观察是，这些常见操作并不是离散孤立点，而是由“是否转移信息”和“如何分配信息”两个连续维度张成的共享空间。\n\n第二部分是搜索空间。EOS 不只搜索 operator regime，还搜索 layer activation 和 retention budget。也就是：哪些层启用压缩、每层保留多少 token、每层的 τ 和门控阈值如何设定，都进入可微优化。为了让离散选择可训练，文中使用连续松弛，把层开关、预算选择等写成概率化或期望形式，在任务损失之外加入效率约束，优化的是单步采样策略下任务损失的期望。\n\n第三部分是搜索目标。整体目标是在给定精度—效率权衡下，最小化任务损失与计算成本的组合。由于基础模型冻结，搜索主要发生在压缩策略参数上，这让搜索成本低于重新训练模型，也更接近部署场景。与 ToMe、SparseVLM 这类方法相比，区别不在于提出一种新的固定压缩规则，而是把多种规则都视作统一空间中的特例，再让数据和约束决定最终组合。","experiments":"从全文可见，实验重点是验证两件事：一是统一空间确实能覆盖已有手工方法；二是搜索得到的混合配置能给出更好的精度—效率折中。论文在代表性的多模态 benchmark 上评估，比较对象包括 pruning、merging 等手工设计基线。结果表明，EOS 一方面能够恢复这些已知策略对应的 operating regime，说明参数化没有偏离已有经验；另一方面，在相同或相近 token/计算预算下，搜索出的 layer-wise hybrid 配置通常优于单一固定算子。\n\n从实验结构看，最值得注意的是消融而不是单点分数。作者分别拆解了：只搜索层位置、只搜索预算、只搜索算子类型，以及三者联合搜索的效果。联合搜索最好，说明压缩层位置、压缩强度和信息转移方式之间存在明显耦合，不能单独拍脑袋设定。另一个重要消融是 τ 与 transfer gate 的作用：τ 控制从 hard merge 到 soft pooling 的连续过渡，gate 控制从直接丢弃到信息保留的切换，这两个参数共同决定了算子行为边界。\n\n给出的全文片段没有完整保留所有具体数字、模型规模和每个 benchmark 的详细表格，因此无法可靠复述精确提升幅度。但从作者的结论和实验组织可以确认，收益主要体现在 trade-off 曲线整体外移，而不是某一个 benchmark 上的偶然增益。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1/2，抓住统一算子空间这个主张：pruning、merge、pool、reweight 不是四套互不相干的方法，而是同一参数化族的不同区域。然后看 Method 里 assignment temperature τ、transfer gate、layer activation、retention budget 这四组变量分别控制什么。这里是全文最有信息量的部分。\n\n如果只花 20 到 30 分钟，建议重点看：1）统一算子公式；2）搜索目标与效率约束；3）主表和消融表。读实验时不要只看谁赢了，而要看搜索是否真的学出了“不同层用不同算子”的异质配置，以及这些配置是否稳定。\n\n值得深挖的点有两个。第一，anchor 选择仍依赖外部 importance score，这意味着 EOS 统一的是“压缩后处理”，而不是从头统一 token selection，本身还有上游启发式依赖。第二，方法建立在冻结模型上，更像 inference-time adaptation；如果把同样的连续算子空间放进预训练或 continued pretraining，是否会学出不同的 token routing 规律，这是后续更有研究味道的方向。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies visual token compression for multimodal foundation models at inference time. Existing methods usually treat pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting as separate hand-designed operators, and then manually choose where to apply them and how aggressively to compress. The actual problem is broader: how to jointly decide when to compress, how many tokens to keep, and how discarded information should be removed or redistributed under an accuracy-efficiency constraint.","importance":"Its relevance to core pretraining is moderate rather than central. The main contribution is a unification and search perspective, not a new pretraining recipe. The useful takeaway for foundation-model researchers is that many token reduction heuristics can be embedded into a continuous operator space and optimized differentiably instead of being fixed by manual design. Non-uniform KV compression effectively preserves more information by considering the individual importance of each KV cache. However, such KV cache heterogeneity introduces various systemic challenges - including memory fragmentation, scheduling complexities, and diminished kernel utilization - which","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2606_06302.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.SE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的不是“如何设计更好的 KV 压缩算法”，而是一个更实际的问题：非均匀 KV cache 压缩在算法上已经显示出比均匀压缩更好的精度-压缩权衡，但一旦放进真实多轮 serving 系统，就会因为内存布局、调度和 kernel 执行三方面的不匹配而失去收益。具体说，现有 vLLM / SGLang 一类系统默认所有 attention head 的 KV 长度一致；而非均匀压缩让不同 head 保留不同长度，结果带来页碎片、运行时回收开销、GPU workload 不均衡，最终使“理论上省下来的 KV”无法转化成吞吐提升。Tangram 的核心问题就是：怎样把 head-wise heterogeneous KV cache 变成一个可被高吞吐 serving 系统稳定利用的对象。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在预训练本身，而在 serving 约束如何反过来影响建模与压缩方法的可落地性。很多 KV 压缩论文只报告 perplexity 或长上下文精度，却默认系统可以无代价地承接异构 cache；Tangram 说明这一步并不成立。它的贡献更像是把“非均匀 KV 压缩”从算法想法推进到系统可部署层面。对做预训练和架构的人，这篇文章的启发是：如果模型内部确实存在稳定的 retrieval heads 或 head-wise retention pattern，那么这些结构性不均匀性不仅可以用于压缩，也可以作为系统 co-design 的先验。换句话说，模型统计规律若足够稳定，就值得在 serving 栈里显式编码，而不是只停留在分析层面。","technical_approach":"$36","experiments":"文中最核心的结果是端到端多轮 serving 吞吐最高提升 2.6×。这个数字的重要性在于，它不是单独某个 kernel microbenchmark，而是作者声称把非均匀 KV 压缩真正转化成了系统级收益。\n\n实验设计围绕三类瓶颈展开：\n1. 内存效率：比较 Tangram 与现有 paged KV 方案在非均匀压缩下的实际可回收显存，重点看 fragmentation 是否显著下降。论文的论证方向很明确：如果没有 head-group page，理论压缩率和实际显存回收之间会有明显落差。\n2. 调度与控制开销：比较动态 reclaim 与 deterministic budget allocation 的 CPU/runtime overhead。作者强调在线跟踪 scattered pages 会拖慢系统，而静态预算能显著降低控制面成本。\n3. It addresses a more practical gap: non-uniform KV compression often looks better than uniform compression in accuracy-vs-memory trade-offs, but existing LLM serving stacks cannot realize those gains efficiently. Current systems such as vLLM and SGLang are built around a uniform-KV assumption, where all heads share the same cache shape. Once different heads retain different numbers of tokens, the system runs into three coupled issues: page fragmentation, high runtime reclamation overhead, and poor GPU kernel utilization due to workload skew. Image tokens have strong local spatial dependencies. PJD exploits this structure at inference time without retraining the model. This is more relevant to decoding and serving than to pretraining itself, but it matters for AR visual token models and unified multimodal LMs that rely on discrete image tokens.","technical_approach":"Conventional Jacobi decoding initializes a future token window after a confirmed prefix and iteratively refines all draft tokens in parallel until part of the window stabilizes. As the one-dimensional window grows, later positions attend to more uncertain draft tokens, which slows convergence. Parallel Jacobi Decoding changes the expansion geometry. Instead of extending only along the flattened sequence, it initializes draft tokens in the next image row once enough spatial context is available, then refines multiple spatial regions in parallel. The method modifies the attention mask to reduce the influence of unstable candidates while preserving the autoregressive dependency pattern. It is training-free and does not require a separate draft model, which distinguishes it from speculative decoding.","experiments":"The method is evaluated on Lumina-mGPT and LlamaGen. Stochasticity is essential in scenarios where multiple actions are equally valid, such as recommending travel itineraries or daily schedules where multiple options have similar utility. In such settings, deterministic policies may lead to repetitive behaviors and reduced coverage of valid alternatives. This paper asks whether MLLMs can follow an instruction to “choose randomly” when all options are semantically equivalent, or whether their output distribution collapses onto a few options in a stable, biased way—especially under multimodal (vision) cues.","importance":"This is an evaluation contribution. For pretraining/alignment researchers, it provides a distributional-behavior diagnostic: (1) instruction tuning/RLHF may implicitly push models toward deterministic, overconfident policies, which hurts coverage and exploration in agent-like settings; the paper claims stronger/more aligned models can show more severe collapse. (2) It surfaces endogenous priors (position/number/visual-saliency biases) that likely arise from pretraining data and decoding conventions rather than prompt artifacts. (3) It suggests that if true stochastic policies are desired, training objectives may need explicit entropy/calibration constraints or logic-neutral randomness supervision.","technical_approach":"RandomBench contains 200 logic-neutral instances split into RB-Text and RB-Vision. Each instance presents K equivalent options and explicitly asks the model to pick randomly. The protocol uses exhaustive repeated sampling: 50 runs per instance to estimate the empirical choice distribution. Three entropy/bias metrics are introduced: Randomness Index (RI) for closeness to uniformity, Bias Intensity Index (BII) for concentration/strength of preference, and Bias Consistency Index (BCI) for stability of the preference across repeats/conditions. The analysis names two failure modes: stochastic collapse (non-uniform, concentrated choice distributions) and visual hijacking (visual saliency/position/geometry overriding the randomness instruction). Control tests such as option-label substitution and cross-lingual prompting are used to argue the bias is endogenous rather than a superficial lexical formatting effect; models also generate fluent post-hoc rationalizations for arbitrary choices.","experiments":"Setup: 200 instances with 50 repeated samples each. Reported findings: stochastic collapse appears across multiple MLLMs in both text-only and vision-language settings; vision inputs exacerbate the issue via visual hijacking; stronger/more instruction-aligned models can exhibit more pronounced and consistent collapse, often with stronger rationalizations. 指标。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper isolates lexical density as a missing variable in long-context failures. It shows that advertised context length and standard needle-style evaluations can overstate usable context capacity when the haystack is redundant. 从单一攻击成功率，拆成普通使用倾向、攻击可诱导性、以及两者之间的差距。这对开源预训练语料审计、版权风险分析、PII 风险评估和 continual pretraining 后的回归测试都有实际意义。","technical_approach":"方法包含两个部分。第一是 PropMe，一个 propensity-aware memorization evaluation evaluated only as a capability elicited by adversarial prefixes, or also as a propensity: whether a model tends to reproduce training data during ordinary, non-adversarial use.","importance":"The contribution is useful for pretraining data governance and continual-pretraining audits. Prefix attacks answer whether a model can be forced to emit memorized text, but they do not estimate the risk under normal prompting. PropMe separates ordinary leakage propensity from adversarially elicited memorization capability. This distinction matters for copyright analysis, privacy review, training-data deduplication policies, and regression testing after continued training on a new corpus.","technical_approach":"The framework has two components. PropMe defines a multi-level evaluation protocol ranging from generic prompts with low lexical overlap to the training set, intended to measure propensity, to prefix attacks that condition the model on prefixes from training examples, intended to measure capability. The paper also introduces a transformation that converts standard memorization metrics into propensity-aware metrics by conditioning the measured behavior on the prompting regime. SimpleTrace is the tracing component. It is built on infini-gram and searches model generations against large-scale training corpora to provide deterministic attribution. It supports verbatim matches, near-verbatim matches, and propensity-transformed memorization scores. Compared with probabilistic detectors or web-snippet matching, the key advantage is direct attribution to source documents in the actual training corpus.","experiments":"The evaluation uses two fully open models: Comma, a monolingual English model, and DFM Decoder, a Danish model continually trained from Comma. The setup compares memorization against the original corpus and the newly introduced continual-pretraining corpus. The reported analysis focuses on the gap between non-adversarial prompts and prefix attacks, and on how continued training changes the source of memorized generations. SimpleTrace enables the authors to separate exact reproduction, near reproduction, and propensity-adjusted leakage estimates.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 25 to 35 minutes. 形态。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者构建了一个公开的 CUA-HandCrafted benchmark。它不是再做一个自动攻击器，而是把已有红队论文中的攻击方法按 prose description 手工转写成可执行模板，放进统一浏览器环境里评测。基准包含 793 个 episode、24 个多步网页任务、56 个攻击模板、8 个攻击家族、5 个攻击深度、4 种 system prompt 配置，并使用 screenshot+a11y 的混合感知。评测指标兼容 AgentDojo 风格的 BU/UuA/ASR/Safety 指标。\n\n设计上最关键的地方有三处。第一，作者刻意区分“技术类别”与“优化 phrasing”。他们不做 RL 搜索，而是只测人类可读、可公开复现的模板，因此测到的是 technique floor，而不是 RL-optimized ceiling。第二，他们做 prompt ablation，把 system prompt 从较强防护版本逐步削到 L0_bare 和 L1_helpful，检查抗攻击性是否仍存在，以判断防护主要来自 prompt 还是模型权重。第三，他们做跨域对照：同样的 frontier 权重，一边放在 browser benchmark，一边放在 coding-agent 的 SkillBench 上，直接测 domain transfer 是否成立。\n\n此外，作者还补了两个边界实验。一个是小预算 RL attacker 上界：在同一 harness 里跑 AutoInject 式 adaptive-random-suffix RL baseline，限定 5 query/pair 和约 10 美元预算，测在这个预算下 RL 是否已经能打开缺口。另一个是 image-channel replication，复现实验性地检查 popup overlay 和页面内恶意文本这两类视觉/图文混合注入在 frontier 模型上是否仍有效。\n\n和已有工作相比，这篇的创新不在攻击算法，而在评测设计和复现审计。它把“旧论文 headline ASR 很高”拆成了几个更可检验的因素：目标模型是否已退役、优化字符串是否公开、手工近似是否还能生效、以及成功是否只局限在某个工具域。","experiments":"$3b","reading_guide":"如果只想抓住结论，先看 Introduction 和 Section 3。这里已经把最关键的数字给全了：browser 0/140、coding 最高 100%/79%、prompt ablation 后仍 0%、小预算 RL 仍 0/100。读到这里就能明白作者真正想修正的是文献解读方式，而不是单纯给出一个新 benchmark。\n\n然后看 benchmark 设计部分，重点看 attack template 是如何从原论文 prose 转写的，哪些地方属于“忠实复现”，哪些地方不可避免要做人工补全。因为整篇工作的说服力建立在这个边界上：它测的是 hand-crafted reproducibility，不是原论文 RL attacker 的理论上限。\n\n接着看 Section 5 的 browser-vs-coding 对照实验。这部分最值得细读，因为它直接支持 domain-conditioned safety 的主张。要看同权重、同类攻击思想、不同工具域下，为什么 coding 面更脆弱；如果文中给了失败案例或 action trace，建议看几条具体轨迹。\n\n最后看 Section 6 和相关表格，尤其是复现审计表。这里能快速判断哪些文献 headline 结果因为模型退役或字符串未公开而难以验证。若做 agent safety eval，这部分比单个 ASR 数字更有方法论意义。\n\n整体属于泛读型论文。读主文 30–40 分钟足够抓住主线；若要复现实验，再看附录里的环境、模板和判分细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses two practical questions. First, do the high prompt-injection success rates reported in recent computer-using-agent red-teaming papers still reproduce on current frontier browser agents? Second, is agent safety a single transferable property, or is it strongly domain-specific? To answer this, the authors build a 793-episode browser benchmark, handcraft attack templates from prior papers, evaluate them on Claude Sonnet 4.6 and GPT-5.4, and then compare the same model weights in a coding-agent setting.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it matters for post-training, safety evaluation, and how we interpret agent hardening. The main value is not a claim that frontier agents are broadly safe. The more useful conclusion is narrower: much of the literature’s high ASR appears to depend on RL-optimized phrasing rather than the attack category alone, and safety improvements are highly domain-conditioned. A model that is robust to hand-crafted browser injection may still be highly vulnerable in a coding-agent environment. For foundation-model researchers, that means safety should not be treated as a single scalar capability, and evidence from one tool domain should not be overgeneralized to another.","technical_approach":"$3c","experiments":"$3d","reading_guide":"$3e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"揭示了模型安全性（抗注入能力）高度依赖于领域（浏览器 vs 代码），说明前沿模型的 safety alignment 泛化性存在严重缺陷，对 post-training 数据配比有直接启示。","en":"Reveals that model safety (injection resistance) is highly domain-dependent (browser vs. coding), highlighting severe generalization flaws in frontier models' safety alignment, which directly informs post-training data mixing."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of California, Berkeley"],"authors":["Nicholas Saban"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Agent Evaluation","Prompt Injection","Safety Alignment","Domain Generalization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05233","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.05233","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.05405","title":"Agents' Last Exam","abstract":"Recent AI systems have achieved strong results on a wide range of benchmarks, yet these gains have not translated into economically meaningful deployment across many professional domains. 强调“可验证交付物”而非主观打分，降低了评测噪声，利于做训练动态/配方迭代（尤其是 agentic 数据的 curriculum、失败模式归因）。\n3) 用 O*NET/SOC 的职业分类做覆盖约束，提供了一个相对系统的“任务分布定义”，便于讨论预训练分布与真实工作分布的错配。\n局限是：它主要是 eval 资产；对预训练研究的价值取决于社区是否把 ALE 行业专家贡献其真实完成过的项目，再经过多轮质检（专家初审→工程 dry-run→专家委员会复审）。这一步的关键取舍是：牺牲规模与采集成本，换取“任务分布更贴近真实工作”。\n- 覆盖定义：以 O*NET/SOC 2018 为锚，形成 13 个行业簇、55 个子领域的 taxonomy，并用该 taxonomy 去映射对比既有 benchmark 的覆盖面（把“覆盖”变成可讨论的量）。\n- 评测对象定位为 GCUA（Generalist Computer-Use Agent）：需要在同一 action loop 里结合视觉感知（GUI）、代码执行/脚本（CLI）、文件操作、工具调用与长时程计划。\n- 验证机制：围绕“交付物/里程碑”做结构化检查（与专家参考产物或可观测指标对齐），目标是把异质输出空间压缩成可自动判定的 checks，减少 human-in-the-loop 评测的不稳定性。\n与 OSWorld（偏 GUI）或 Terminal-Bench（偏 CLI）相比，ALE 的差异在于：任务表面是二者并集，并且强调真实职业工作流与可验证交付物，而不是可快速规模化的合成任务。","experiments":"论文给出的关键信号是“未饱和程度”和“与既有基准的落差”：\n- 覆盖与难度：1K+ 任务实例，55 子领域/13 行业簇，并分难度层级（文中提到 也低于 50%，hardest 低于 10%；其他主流 agent（含 Claude Code）在高难度接近 0。这里的价值主要是“能力断层定位”：短 CLI 基准高分并不意味着端到端职业工作流可用。\n- 论文更像 benchmark paper：消融重点不在模型结构，而在任务设计、验证可行性、覆盖映射与基线差距展示。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟）：\n- As these memories grow, they may reinforce one another, diverge across contexts, or directly conflict, making correct assistance depend on memory relations rather than isolated recall. Existing long-term memory benchmarks rarely probe how agents preserve and utilize such relations during downstream tasks. Existing benchmarks are usually built by human-heavy, benchmark-specific pipelines, and many saturate quickly as model capabilities improve. Benchmark Agent aims to turn a high-level evaluation request into an executable, customizable, and refreshable benchmark with much less manual work.","importance":"The contribution is mainly evaluation infrastructure rather than a modeling or pretraining method. Its relevance to foundation-model research lies in evaluation lifecycle management. As model iteration accelerates, static benchmarks can lose discriminative power shortly after release. A reusable benchmark-building pipeline may help pretraining teams define finer-grained capabilities, generate targeted test sets, and maintain evaluation freshness. The key caution is quality control: automatically generated benchmarks must be checked for judge bias, annotation errors, data leakage, and comparability across model versions.","technical_approach":"Benchmark Agent uses a two-component design. The Benchmark Planner maps user requirements into concrete benchmark specifications, including subtask decomposition, data configuration, and transformation plans. It also checks whether each subtask can be grounded in available data. The Benchmark Executor then turns the plan into a runnable benchmark by using tools for sample generation, annotation, formatting, and quality control. This split is sensible for a long-horizon task: a single end-to-end agent would have to handle high-level task design, data constraints, execution details, and validation in one loop. Separating planning from execution makes the pipeline easier to constrain and audit.","experiments":"The system is used to produce 15 representative benchmarks. Such agents may retrieve public benchmark metadata, question context, or even ground-truth answers via web search. This gives rise to Search-Time Contamination (STC), where external retrieval bypasses intended reasoning and inflates measured performance. We systematically study STC in deep research agent evaluation. We define ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05241.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CR","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 deep research agent 在公开 benchmark 上评测时的 search-time contamination：模型在推理过程中主动联网搜索，可能直接检索到 benchmark 元数据、题目上下文，甚至标准答案，从而绕过原本要测的推理能力，导致分数被系统性抬高。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，但对 post-train agent、联网推理系统和评测协议很重要。它提醒人们：一旦模型具备外部检索和多步搜索能力，传统“公开测试集 + 最终答案打分”的评测假设就不再成立。对于基座研究员，这个问题会反过来影响训练信号质量、RL 目标设计和 benchmark 可信度，因为如果评测被污染，后续基于这些分数做模型选择、数据合成或策略优化都会偏掉。","technical_approach":"论文先把 STC 明确分成三个层级。第一类是 Benchmark Metadata Leakage，即 agent 的搜索行为已经被 benchmark 名称、数据集页面、题库镜像等元信息引导到测试集相关资源上；第二类是 Question-Context Leakage，即检索结果暴露了题目的来源、上下文或近似原题，虽然未必直接给出答案，但已经显著缩小了解题空间；第三类是 Explicit Answer Leakage，即网页中直接出现标准答案或足以唯一确定答案的内容。\n\n在方法上，作者不是训练新 agent，而是做 contamination detection 与 impact quantification。核心做法是记录 agent 的完整搜索轨迹，包括 query、访问 URL、页面内容和回合级行为，然后用检测算法在 question-level 和 turn-level 标注泄漏类型。这样可以分析：某道题是否发生污染、污染发生在第几步、发生后 agent 的行为是否从原推理轨迹切换到检索到的答案。\n\n论文的一个重要点是把 STC 从“数据污染”区分为“推理时污染”。这不是训练集记忆问题，而是 inference-time retrieval 直接把 benchmark artifact 带回上下文。对于 deep research agent，这种污染往往比传统 benchmark contamination 更隐蔽，因为它发生在工具调用链中，而不是静态参数里。","experiments":"实验覆盖六个公开 benchmark，并重点分析医学和临床场景，因为这类题目在公开题库、论坛、教育网站和代码仓库中暴露度高。作者发现所有评测集都存在不同程度的 STC。文中给出的几个数字比较关键：MedMCQA 上接近四分之一的问题可以从网络上检索到答案；在 HLE 的 biological 和 chemical 子集上，STC 带来的性能抬升最高可达 4%；一旦 agent 检索到泄漏答案，不论任务本身难度如何，准确率都会迅速升到 95% 以上，并且推理轨迹会明显偏离原先路径，转而采纳泄漏答案。\n\n作者还检查了商业系统的行为。以 Gemini Deep Research 为例，在抽样的 100 道 MedQA 题目中，agent 有 60% 的情况访问到了同时包含原题和答案的网页。这说明问题不是某个学术原型特有，而是联网 research agent 的普遍评测风险。\n\n实验最有价值的地方不只是报告污染比例，而是把“污染发生后性能如何变化”也量化了：答案泄漏会同时带来更高准确率和更快收敛，说明它确实在替代推理，而不是仅仅提供弱提示。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点看 Introduction、STC 三分类定义、检测算法描述，以及案例分析图。预计 20 分钟左右可以抓住主要结论。\n\n阅读时建议带着两个问题看。第一，作者如何区分 metadata leakage、context leakage 和 explicit answer leakage，边界是否清楚。第二，污染检测是基于页面内容匹配、答案显式出现，还是结合了 agent 行为转折来判定。后者更重要，因为它关系到“检索到了”与“真正利用了”之间的区别。\n\n如果只看一组实验，优先看 MedMCQA、HLE 子集和商业 agent 案例。这几部分最能说明公开 benchmark 在联网 agent 时代的脆弱性。对做训练的人来说，最后关于 sandbox、透明搜索轨迹、私有测试集和动态生成测试实例的建议也值得看，因为这些会直接影响后续 RL 或 agent tuning 的评测闭环。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies search-time contamination (STC) in deep research agents evaluated on public benchmarks. However, these agents remain fundamentally episodic: they fail to retain, refine, and reuse experiences across tasks, repeatedly reconstructing context from scratch and reproducing similar mistakes. Even with memory support, they offer no remedy for the absence of a principled, task-agnostic \\textit{memory utility}, making them difficult to ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05646.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SE","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有的软件工程（SE）Agent 通常是片段式的，缺乏跨任务保留、提炼和重用经验的机制；而现有的记忆增强方法缺乏与下游任务表现挂钩的、任务无关的记忆效用评估标准。","importance":"提出了一个闭环的记忆优化框架 MemOp，将记忆效用严格定义为对下游任务性能的因果提升，为 Agent 记忆机制的评估和无监督优化提供了原则性方法。","technical_approach":"MemOp 包含两个核心创新：1) 将记忆效用定义为双重工具：既是评估基准，也是优化信号，完全基于下游任务的实际表现。2) 闭环记忆优化：通过轨迹级反思从完成的任务中提取候选记忆，然后通过性能验证（测量其对下游结果的因果影响）将候选记忆转化为训练信号，从而在无外部监督的情况下自适应地优化记忆模型。","experiments":"在单回合记忆生成和跨回合记忆演化两种设置下进行了评估。结果表明，MemOp 显著提升了 SE Agent 的性能（成功率绝对提升高达 5.25%），训练后的记忆模型能生成高质量的反思，并具有很强的适应性，同时大幅降低了计算成本（相对降低 $\\ge 9.79\\%$）。","reading_guide":"重点关注第 2 节（MemOp 框架）和第 3 节（记忆效用的定义与闭环优化机制）。理解其如何将“记忆是否有用”转化为可计算的因果影响，这对于设计具备持续学习能力的复杂 Agent 架构非常有价值。","authors_note":"Xuehang Guo (William & Mary), Xingyao Wang (OpenHands)"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Current Software Engineering (SE) agents are episodic, lacking mechanisms to retain, refine, and reuse experience across tasks. Existing memory-augmented approaches lack a task-agnostic utility metric tied to downstream performance.","importance":"Proposes MemOp, a closed-loop memory optimization framework that strictly defines memory utility as the causal improvement on downstream task performance, providing a principled approach for evaluating and unsupervisedly optimizing agent memory mechanisms.","technical_approach":"MemOp introduces two key innovations: 1) Principled memory utility as a dual harness: serving as both an evaluation benchmark and an optimization signal, grounded entirely in downstream performance. 2) Closed-loop memory optimization: candidate memories are distilled from completed trajectories via reflection, and then validated by measuring their causal impact on downstream outcomes. This validation converts candidates into training signals, enabling adaptive memory optimization without external supervision.","experiments":"Evaluated under single-episode memory generation and cross-episode memory evolution settings. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05384.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是 LLM-as-judge 一个此前很少被单独刻画的失效模式：判决已经给出之后，是否还能被后续对话系统性改写。作者把这个问题和常见的“稳定性”区分开来。稳定性指同一输入重复评测、或在中性重问下是否保持一致；而他们关心的“post-decision manipulability”指在候选答案完全不变的前提下，仅通过后续与 judge 的交互，就让原本的胜负判断翻转。核心问题不是 judge 会不会有随机波动，而是它是否缺乏对抗性鲁棒性，导致评测结果并不是固定属性，而是可被事后操纵的对话产物。","importance":"对预训练本身没有直接方法贡献，但对后训练、对齐和评测基础设施有现实影响。现在很多模型迭代、偏好优化、reward model 校验、公开榜单比较，都依赖 LLM judge。如果 judge 在“重复评测时稳定”，却在“带目标的追问”下容易翻案，那么现有自动评测只测到了可重复性，没有测到抗操纵性。这个区分很重要，因为它会影响两类事情：一是 benchmark 排名是否稳，二是用 judge 作为训练信号时，信号是否容易被策略性利用。对做 RLHF、RLAIF、judge distillation、model grading 的人，这篇文章提供了一个更接近安全性和机制层面的评测视角。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者设计了一套受控的 post-decision interaction protocol。实验从 MT-Bench 和 AlpacaEval 中抽取 response pair，先让 judge 给出 baseline 判决，然后在保持候选回答不变的情况下，施加不同类型的后续交互：重复评测、neutral reconsideration，以及带明确目标的 persuasive challenge。这里最关键的是因果隔离：被评测对象固定，只改变 judge 后续接收到的话语，从而把“判决翻转”归因到交互本身，而不是输入样本变化。\n\n他们提出了几类 challenge，其中最重要的是 anti-baseline challenge：后续对话明确站在 baseline 反方向，要求 judge 重新考虑原先输的一方是否更好。为了避免只看到表面翻转，作者又做了 counterbalanced target audit，用对称设计区分“单纯重新思考带来的自然波动”和“被定向说服带来的额外偏移”。文中用 persuasion-induced reversibility 和 target-directed steering 两个量来拆分这两部分效应。结果显示，确实存在显著的可逆性，但净定向 steering 很弱，说明很多翻转不是稳定地朝某一目标被控制，而是 judge 在受挑战后进入高可塑状态。\n\n机制分析部分也比较完整。作者检查了 judge 的自报置信度，发现高置信并不意味着抗翻转；很多后来被推翻的判决，初始置信度仍然很高，说明 calibration 失真。作者还比较了不同 persuasion framing，发现 authority framing 比 reasoning-based challenge 更有效，也就是说“以权威口吻要求重审”比“给出更扎实的论证”更容易改判。最后，他们分析翻案前后的 justification overlap，发现 judge 改判后常会生成与原先理由重叠很低的新解释，更像事后合理化，而不是显式承认先前推理哪里错了。\n\n为了量化这种脆弱性，文中提出 Evaluation Robustness Score, ERS，用来衡量一个 judge 在后决策交互下的抗操纵能力。ERS 不是传统 accuracy 指标，而是把评测器本身当作被测对象，关注其在交互扰动下的稳定边界。","experiments":"实验主要基于 MT-Bench 和 AlpacaEval 的成对比较。最醒目的结果是：在重复评测和中性重问条件下，judge 的判决总体稳定；但在 anti-baseline challenge 下，49% 的原始判决会翻转。这说明“稳定”与“鲁棒”不是一回事。\n\n作者进一步做了 counterbalanced audit，得到 persuasion-induced reversibility P_S = 0.194，说明定向挑战确实会显著增加翻案概率；但 signed target-direction effect D_S^signed = -0.018，clipped D_S = 0，说明这种影响更多体现为把 judge 从原判中撬松，而不是可靠地把它推向某个指定方向。换句话说，judge 容易被动摇，但不一定能被精确操控。\n\n更关键的是翻案质量。文中指出，在 targeted post-decision challenge 触发的 reversals 中，64% 是 harmful reversals，也就是改判后与人类偏好更不一致。这一点直接说明后续交互并没有在多数情况下纠正错误，反而更常破坏对齐。作者还展示了这种翻转会传导到 benchmark ranking，意味着榜单名次可能受 challenge protocol 影响，而不是只由模型输出质量决定。\n\n机制实验里，authority framing 的效果强于 reasoning framing；而且即便 judge 在改判后自报置信下降，翻案仍会发生，说明它不是“更谨慎后修正错误”，而更像“在社会性提示下重写立场”。另外，justification overlap 低，支持 post hoc rationalization 的解释。文中还分析了 multi-step persuasion dynamics、inter-judge agreement 和 difficulty 对脆弱性的影响，整体结论是：难样本更容易被撬动，但这种脆弱性并不只存在于边界样本。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但建议把问题定义和实验设计看仔细。优先看 Introduction 里对 stability 与 robustness 的区分，这是全文最重要的概念切口。然后看 challenge protocol 的具体构造，尤其是 anti-baseline challenge 和 counterbalanced target audit，这决定了实验结论是否站得住。\n\n结果部分重点看几张主表：一张比较 repeated / neutral / challenge 条件下 reversal rate；一张拆 persuasion-induced reversibility 与 target steering；再看 harmful reversal、human alignment 和 ranking impact 的结果。机制分析里，建议重点看 confidence calibration、authority vs reasoning framing、以及 justification overlap 这三块，它们解释了为什么 judge 会在“看起来很稳”的同时又“很容易被撬动”。\n\n如果阅读时间有限，30 分钟左右可以抓住主结论；如果要判断它对训练管线的影响，建议再花时间想两个延伸问题：第一，能否把 ERS 纳入 judge 选择标准；第二，若用 LLM judge 产生偏好数据，训练出的 policy 会不会学会利用这种可操纵性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper isolates a failure mode in LLM-as-judge systems that is different from ordinary prompt sensitivity or run-to-run variance: once a judgment has already been made, can later interaction overturn it while the evaluated candidate responses remain fixed? The authors call this post-decision manipulability. Their key point is that evaluation pipelines often check stability under repetition, but rarely test robustness against targeted conversational pressure after the decision. A judge may look reproducible in standard settings and still be highly reversible under adversarial follow-up.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for the infrastructure around model development. LLM judges are now used in benchmark ranking, preference data generation, reward modeling, and model iteration loops. If a judge is stable under neutral reevaluation yet vulnerable to post-hoc challenge, then current automated evaluation captures reproducibility but not resistance to manipulation. That distinction matters for both leaderboard credibility and training-signal quality. It provides a low-cost new observation signal for reward hacking detection in alignment training and inference monitoring, and verifies the effectiveness of self-commitment timing as a behavioral probe.","technical_approach":"The self-commitment latency metric is defined as follows: for a single generated full chain of thought, truncate prefixes of different lengths at a fixed stride, force the model to output answers based on each prefix, calculate the match rate between prefix answers and the model's own final answer from the full generation, and plot the commitment curve against chain of thought length. The core statistic is the prefix length when the match rate first reaches 0.8 (i.e., 4 out of 5 samples match), which distinguishes honest reasoning from prompt-anchored cheating: in cheating scenarios the model can lock in the final answer very early in the chain of thought, while honest reasoning only achieves stable commitment after intermediate derivation steps.","experiments":"Paired controlled experiments are conducted on 50 integer-answer problems from GSM8K using the Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct-4bit model. Each problem is run twice: once with a normal prompt and once with a prompt appended with the correct answer as a hint. Results show that the prefix length for first reaching 0.8 commitment in the hinted cheating scenario is significantly shorter than in the honest reasoning scenario, and the overall commitment curve has lower uncertainty. Threshold sensitivity tests across values from 0.5 to 1.0 and stratified checks on correct samples rule out confounding factors such as threshold artifacts or the signal only detecting incorrect answers. The experiments also confirm that the original backtracking-mass hypothesis produces weak signals.","reading_guide":"Focus on the metric definition and experiment pipeline design in Chapter 2, as well as the paired experiment plots and threshold sensitivity ablation in Chapter 3. While self-supervised learning (SSL) has yielded strong domain-specific encoders like speech or music experts, multi-domain approaches like USAD and SPEAR remain limited in coverage and evaluation. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_06444.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["eess.AS","cs.CL","cs.SD"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是通用音频编码器的知识整合问题：单一 SSL 音频模型通常只擅长 speech、general audio 或 music 中的一个域，而音频 LLM 又需要一个覆盖多域、可直接接入下游大模型的前端编码器。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在跨域表示蒸馏。USAD 2.0 不是重新设计音频 tokenizer，也不是端到端训练 audio LLM，而是把多个领域专家编码器的隐层知识压到一个统一 encoder 中。这个思路和 LLM 预训练中的数据配比、teacher mixture、domain routing 很接近：不同数据域不应无差别地对齐所有 teacher，而应根据输入域调整蒸馏信号强度。第二阶段引入监督式音频 foundation encoder，也说明纯 SSL 表示未必最适合作为 LLM 前端；面向 LLM 接入时，ASR、captioning 等监督信号可能提供更直接的语义对齐。","technical_approach":"方法从 USAD 的 layer-wise representation distillation 出发。原始 USAD 把 speech SSL teacher 和 general-audio SSL teacher 的多层 hidden states 蒸馏到一个 student encoder 中，所有 teacher loss 等权。USAD 2.0 的关键改动是 domain-aware distillation：如果样本来自某个已知音频域，就提高对应 teacher 的 loss 权重，同时保留其他 teacher 的较小权重。论文用一个系数 alpha 控制 matched teacher 和 mismatched teacher 的权重比；alpha=1 退化为普通均匀蒸馏，alpha 趋近无穷则接近只蒸馏域内 teacher。作者选择软权重而不是硬路由，是因为音频域之间有重叠，例如 speech 常出现在复杂环境声中，speech teacher 的去噪和语音结构知识仍可能帮助 mixed audio。第二个改动是加入 music teacher 和音乐数据，补齐 USAD 在音乐任务上的短板。第三个改动是 USAD 2.0+：先做 SSL teacher 的多域蒸馏，再用强监督音频 encoder 做第二阶段蒸馏，使 encoder 更贴近音频 LLM、检索、语音编码等使用场景。最后，论文把模型扩到 1B 参数，采用降低时间分辨率并加深网络的方式控制计算成本。","experiments":"论文评估覆盖 probing 和 LLM-based evaluation 两类。前者检查 encoder 表示在 speech、general audio、music 任务上的线性可用性；后者更接近真实音频 LLM 前端的使用方式。关键实验点包括：加入 music teacher 后，音乐相关任务如 genre、key classification 的短板得到改善；domain-aware distillation 相比等权蒸馏更稳，因为它减少了 teacher-domain mismatch 带来的干扰；第二阶段监督蒸馏提升了面向 LLM 应用的可用性；1B 深度扩展版本在保持成本可控的同时进一步提高整体表现。文中还通过消融和可视化分析说明 alpha、teacher 组合、第二阶段监督 teacher 对最终表示的影响。给定内容中没有提供完整表格数值，精读时应以主实验表和 ablation 表为准核对具体提升幅度。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20-30 分钟。优先看方法部分的 domain-aware 部分可以快速看，重点不是架构新意，而是作者如何在音频序列较长的条件下降低时间分辨率、用 depth scaling 换取容量。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical representation-learning problem for audio LLMs: a single audio frontend has to cover speech, general audio, and music, while most existing SSL encoders are specialized for only one of these domains.","importance":"The main value is in the training recipe rather than in a new model family. USAD 2.0 treats universal audio representation learning as a multi-teacher distillation problem with domain-dependent loss weighting. This is relevant to pretraining because it mirrors a broader issue in foundation models: heterogeneous data sources and heterogeneous teachers should not necessarily contribute uniform gradients. The second-stage distillation from supervised audio encoders is also informative. latent","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05769.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是视频事件预测里一个很具体但也很典型的问题：模型需要根据部分视频去推断未来会发生什么，而现有视频 MLLM 往往把中间推理先翻译成文本。这样做便于自回归生成，却会丢掉运动、几何关系、交互顺序这类未来预测真正依赖的细粒度视觉结构。论文试图给模型一个更合适的中间推理接口：在文本 token 之间插入连续 latent visual span，让模型在生成过程中交替使用语言和潜在视觉状态来“想象未来”。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关系不如纯语言建模强，但它提供了两个有启发的点。第一，连续 latent 作为中间推理通道，未必只适合静态图像问答，也可以扩展到时间结构更强的视频未来建模。第二，作者把 SFT 冷启动、数据筛选和 latent-aware RL 串成一个闭环，说明这类新推理接口往往不能只靠监督学习学出来，训练信号设计同样关键。对于关心多模态统一建模、latent reasoning、以及 pretrain/post-train 接口的人，这篇更像一个有代表性的系统化 recipe，而不是单点技巧。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Future-L1。模型基于标准视频 MLLM 骨干，额外引入一个 latent visual reasoning channel，并定义三个特殊标记：<|latent_start|>、<|latent|>、<|latent_end|>。生成开始于文本模式；一旦模型输出 latent_start，后续每个 latent 位置不再经过词表投影，而是直接把该位置 hidden state 作为连续 latent，并把它反馈为下一步输入 embedding。这样 latent span 内部形成一个连续状态自回归过程，同时这些状态保留在 KV cache 中，供后续文本推理读取。span 长度可变，并允许一次回答中出现多个 latent span，使模型能按需分配“视觉思考”预算。\n\n训练分两阶段。第一阶段是 SFT 冷启动。作者从 TwiFF-2.7M 中构造 Future-L1-50K，不是简单抽样，而是做 visual-gain curation：对每个候选样本，用 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 比较“只看观测前缀”与“额外给中间未来帧提示”两种条件下的预测效果，只保留那些未来视觉提示确实带来边际收益的样本。这样筛掉两类无效数据：一类是不需要未来视觉也能答对的简单样本，另一类是即便给未来帧也无帮助的噪声样本。SFT 时，模型既学习何时触发 latent span，也把 latent hidden states 对齐到未来帧 embedding，给 RL 提供可用初始轨迹。\n\n第二阶段是 LA-DAPO，一种 latent-aware RL 目标。它不只奖励最终答案对错，还对采样得到的 latent trajectory 施加 outcome-contrastive 和 temporal-diversity 奖励。前者鼓励那些能导向正确预测的 latent 未来轨迹，后者抑制重复、塌缩的视觉思维过程。这个设计的意图很明确：如果只看最终答案，latent span 很容易退化成可有可无的中间噪声；加入对 latent 轨迹质量和多样性的约束，模型才更可能真正利用连续视觉通道。","experiments":"结果主要集中在两个 benchmark。FutureBench 上，Future-L1-RL 把 Qwen3-VL-8B 从 61.0 提升到 85.4，超过文中对比的前一最好方法 Video-CoE 10.4 分。TwiFF-Bench 上，平均分从 2.44 提升到 3.04。更关键的是训练范式对比：在相同 curated data source 下，text-only SFT 只能到 65.0，而 interleaved latent SFT 达到 73.2，说明收益不只是“多了监督数据”，而是中间推理模态更匹配未来视觉结构。全文主张 RL 进一步把 latent 轨迹从“可用”推到“更稳定、更有效”，但从研究判断上，更值得注意的是数据筛选和 latent interface 本身已经带来明显增益；RL 是在这个接口成立之后继续放大效果。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点看三块。先看 Method 里 latent span 的生成机制，理解它和普通文本 CoT、以及一次性视觉 hint 的区别。再看 Future-L1-50K 的 visual-gain curation，这部分决定了作者为什么不用全量 TwiFF，而要按“未来帧是否真的有边际帮助”来筛数据。最后看 SFT 与 LA-DAPO 的对比实验，尤其是 text-only SFT、interleaved latent SFT、以及 RL 之后的差距，能帮助判断性能提升分别来自接口、数据还是优化目标。预计 25 到 35 分钟可以读完主线；如果你关心 latent reasoning 是否真的被模型使用，还应留意文中是否提供了足够强的因果诊断，这一点相较于更偏分析型的工作仍然偏弱。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in video event prediction. The model must infer unseen future states from a partial video, yet most video MLLMs externalize intermediate reasoning in text. That interface is convenient for autoregressive decoding but poorly matched to future visual dynamics, because motion, geometry, and interaction cues are compressed into language too early. The proposed solution is to let the model reason through interleaved continuous visual latents during generation, so that language can organize the process while latent spans preserve future-oriented visual structure.","importance":"Its relevance to pretraining is indirect but meaningful. The paper shows that continuous latent reasoning channels are not limited to static image QA; they can be extended to temporally structured prediction where the intermediate state should represent evolving future visuals. It also presents a full training recipe rather than a single trick: curated supervision for cold start, a dedicated latent interface, and latent-aware RL for trajectory refinement. For researchers interested in multimodal pretraining, latent reasoning, or the pretrain-to-post-train interface, the useful takeaway is that new reasoning channels often need matched data selection and optimization signals, not just architectural insertion.","technical_approach":"$48","experiments":"The main results are reported on two benchmarks. On FutureBench, Future-L1-RL improves Qwen3-VL-8B from 61.0 to 85.4, outperforming the previous best method cited in the paper, Video-CoE, by 10.4 points. On TwiFF-Bench, the average score rises from 2.44 to 3.04. The more informative comparison is within the same curated data source: text-only SFT reaches 65.0, while interleaved-latent SFT reaches 73.2. That gap suggests the gain is not merely due to extra supervision, but to using an intermediate modality better aligned with future visual structure. The paper further argues that RL improves latent trajectory quality beyond SFT. From a research perspective, the strongest evidence is that the latent interface plus data curation already matter substantially, while RL amplifies the effect once the interface is usable.","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Read the method section first to understand how latent spans differ from ordinary text chain-of-thought and from one-shot visual hints. Finally, compare text-only SFT, interleaved latent SFT, and RL-enhanced results to separate the contributions of interface design, data selection, and optimization. i","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_06188.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文想找一个不依赖额外训练探针、直接来自模型内部状态的“推理强度信号”。已有工作要么从输出侧看 CoT、熵、正确率相关性，要么用 SAE 这类外部工具去挖 reasoning feature，但后者训练成本高、依赖模型和数据。论文要回答的是：LLM 在层间是否存在稳定的推理动态，以及这种动态能否用一个内生、训练无关的量来刻画。作者给出的答案是隐藏状态的 ℓ2 范数。","importance":"对预训练 recipe 的直接影响有限，但对训练动态分析、机制解释、test-time compute 分配和 reasoning control 有启发。更具体地说，它提出了一个极低成本的内部诊断量：layer-wise hidden-state norm。若这个信号在更多模型上成立，它可以成为分析“模型何时在认真算、哪些层在承担推理负荷”的通用仪表，而不必每次先训练 SAE 或做复杂探针。对基座研究员而言，价值主要在机制分析与推理时干预，而不是提升预训练本身。","technical_approach":"技术路线分三步。第一步，用 SAE 作为诊断工具而不是最终信号。作者先在多个层上训练 SAE，把隐藏表示分解成稀疏特征，识别与 reasoning 相关的 feature，并观察这些 feature 在深层尤其是后段层显著增强。这个阶段的作用是建立一个较可信的“推理活跃层”参照系。\n\n第二步，建立 hidden-state ℓ2 norm 与 reasoning feature activation 的理论联系。论文在若干条件下给出双边界：如果 SAE 重构足够 faithful，且 reasoning feature 与 semantic feature 近似正交，则隐藏状态范数可以界定 reasoning feature 的激活强度。直觉上，推理会把表示推向更高能量、更大幅度的子空间，因此 norm 会抬升。这里的理论不是严格现实世界定理，而是一个带条件的解释框架：它说明为什么 norm 可能是 reasoning intensity 的代理，而不是仅仅经验相关。\n\n第三步，把这个信号用于 test-time control。作者提出三种无训练、无额外数据的方法。其一是 Adaptive Layer-wise Reasoning Recursion，在高推理强度层分配额外计算，类似只对关键层做更深处理。其二是 Endogenous Reasoning State Steering，把隐藏状态朝高强度子空间引导，以增强中间计算。其三是 ℓ2-guided Response Selection，在多个候选输出中选取 reasoning intensity 更高的答案。三者共同特点是都不改模型参数，而是利用 norm 作为在线控制信号。\n\n论文还做了相关性与因果验证。相关性上，比较 ℓ2 norm 与 SAE reasoning feature、输出熵等指标的对齐程度，并声称 norm 优于其他简单统计量。因果上，在 reasoning-heavy 层压制高范数 token，比随机压制带来更大性能下降，作为“高范数位置更关键”的证据。","experiments":"实验结论有三层。第一，表征分析显示 reasoning feature 在后层集中增强，说明推理并非均匀分布在所有层，而更像在中后段进入高活跃状态。第二，相关性实验表明 layer-wise ℓ2 norm 与 SAE 识别的 reasoning feature activation、以及输出不确定性指标有较强对齐，而且优于若干替代统计量。第三，基于 norm 的 test-time 方法在多个数学和通用推理 benchmark 上平均提升 4.51%，在更难的推理任务上提升 9.13%。\n\n更值得注意的是因果干预结果：对 reasoning-heavy 层的高范数 token 做抑制，比随机抑制更伤性能，这支持“高范数不是伴随现象，而与有效推理计算有关”。不过这类证据仍然是局部的。它说明 norm 是有用信号，但还不能证明所有高范数都对应正确推理，也不能排除它有时只是高不确定性、高冲突或高激活密度的混合指标。\n\n从论文定位看，数字提升不是最重要部分。更关键的是它把一个简单可测的内部量和 reasoning dynamics 连接起来，并展示了这个量可以驱动实际干预。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，明确两层问题：一是 reasoning 在层间怎么展开，二是能否找到内生信号。然后直接看 SAE 分析部分的层间激活图，确认作者观察到的“后层推理增强”现象。接着看理论部分，重点不是逐式推导，而是弄清楚结论依赖哪些假设：faithful reconstruction、feature 近似正交、reasoning/semantic 分解是否可信。\n\n实验部分建议按顺序看：相关性比较、因果抑制实验、三种 test-time 方法的主结果。若时间有限，优先看 high-norm suppression 和 response selection，这两部分最容易判断信号是否有操作价值。\n\n预计 25 分钟左右可以完成有效泛读。阅读时要带着保留意见：hidden-state norm 很可能是一个混合信号，既包含 reasoning，也包含不确定性、冲突、长度效应和层归一化后的几何偏置。值得继续追问的是，这个现象在不同架构、不同归一化方式、不同 CoT 风格模型上是否稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper looks for a model-intrinsic, The question here is whether reasoning has a stable layer-wise signature and whether that signature can be captured directly from the model’s own hidden states. We trace this leakage to two architectural components, The causal mask is responsible for the first: its per-query softmax denominator depends on the absolute query position by construction. The residual stream supplies the second. Under causal attention the activation at position $0$ attends only to itself and runs as a closed dynamical system","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_06160.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的问题很具体：decoder-only Transformer 在使用 RoPE 时，按设计注意力 logit 只应依赖相对位置差 i-j，但实际模型的注意力模式却能稳定泄露绝对位置。作者不是停留在“观察到泄露”这一层，而是追问泄露究竟来自哪里、通过什么路径进入注意力计算、不同架构里两类来源各占多少。全文给出的结论是，绝对位置信息主要来自两个结构性来源：一是 causal mask 让 softmax 分母随 query 的绝对位置变化，从而即使 logits 只编码相对位移，attention weight 仍带上绝对位置；二是 residual stream 本身在层间传播中形成了位置相关状态，后续 head 通过 query/key 投影把这些状态重新读入注意力。作者进一步指出，很多人直觉上怀疑的 attention sink 并不是“内容存储器”，它更像一个 token-anchored 的锚点，真正可解码的绝对位置信号主要来自 position 0 在因果注意力下形成的封闭动力系统，以及 downstream 的 sink-reading heads 对这一路径的读取。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要有三层。第一，它把“RoPE 只编码相对位置”这件事从公式层面的性质，和“训练后模型实际是否只用相对位置”区分开了。对长上下文外推、位置泛化、RoPE 变体设计、sliding-window attention、NTK scaling 等工作，这个区分很关键，因为很多失败或收益可能不是来自 RoPE 本身，而是来自 mask 与 residual dynamics 的联合作用。第二，它给出了一个可操作的 leakage 分解框架：把绝对位置泄露拆成 causal-mask 分量和 residual-stream 分量。这个分解对分析训练动态、解释 attention sink、设计更“纯相对”的架构都有直接价值。第三，它提醒做架构和位置编码实验时，不能只看 attention logits 的理论不变性；softmax、mask、BOS 处理方式、position 0 的特殊动力学都会改变模型最终学到的位置信号。对基座模型训练来说，这属于“实现细节会反过来塑造归纳偏置”的典型例子。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个关键点。第一，作者定义了一个比线性回归更稳健的 absolute-position leakage 度量。因为 i、j、i-j 线性相关，而且 RoPE 的相对位置信号本来就是多频余弦结构，如果直接用线性项拟合，很容易把相对位移误判成绝对位置。为此他们先用一个饱和的 relative-offset baseline 拟合每个 i-j 的自由参数，再在 full model 中加入中心化后的 i 和 j，额外解释到的 ΔR² 被视为绝对位置泄露。这个设计避免了把 RoPE 的正常相对结构错算成 leakage。第二，作者用 bidirectional attention ablation 去隔离 causal-mask 分量。思路是：如果把因果 mask 去掉，softmax 分母对每个 query 的可见 token 数就不再依赖绝对位置，那么剩余的 ΔR² 主要来自 residual stream。由 baseline ΔR² 与 bidirectional ΔR² 的比值，可以近似分解两类来源的占比。作者也很谨慎地说明这不是严格可加分解，因为 forward pass 变了，但通过 identity-RoPE 和 scrambled-RoPE 等干预得到近似一致的比例，说明这个近似在实验里是可信的。第三，作者沿着 residual-stream 路径继续追因。他们发现 position 0 在 causal attention 下只能看自己，因此每一层的 x_0 都是由初始 token embedding 驱动的封闭递推系统；这条轨迹会被某些 sink-reading heads 读出，再传播给别的位置。重要的是，attention sink 本身并不携带丰富可解码内容：高注意力位置的 topic probe 接近随机，真正能解码位置的信号往往出现在注意力权重很小但稳定存在的通道里。作者还做了 BOS 位置移动和 BOS 替换实验，证明 sink 是 token-anchored 而不是“固定在绝对位置 0 的神秘缓存”。不同架构上，NTK scaling 会压低 residual-stream 分量，sliding-window attention 会削弱 causal-mask 分量，因此两类泄露的平衡是架构相关的，而不是单一普适机制。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，实验重点不是追求大规模 benchmark，而是做机制拆解。作者研究了三类架构，并在每类上测量 baseline leakage、bidirectional ablation 后的 leakage，以及禁用 RoPE 的对照。核心结果有几项。其一，三类模型都存在可测的绝对位置泄露，说明这不是某个实现偶然性。其二，bidirectional ablation 后 leakage 仍显著存在，证明 residual stream 是独立来源，而不是全部由 causal softmax 造成。其三，identity-RoPE 与 scrambled-RoPE 得到近似相同的 causal-mask / residual-stream 比例，支持“残差流贡献是单一路径”这一解释。其四，attention sink 的高权重并不等于高信息量：最高注意力位置的 topic-probe 准确率接近 chance，而可解码位置的注意力强度比 sink 小约三个数量级。其五，移动 BOS 到 position 8 时，sink 跟着 BOS 移动；把 BOS 换成随机罕见 token 后，sink 显著减弱，说明 sink 依赖 token 身份。其六，架构差异方面，NTK scaling 会抑制 residual-stream 贡献，sliding-window attention 会减弱 causal-mask 贡献。摘要截断了部分具体数字，因此这里能确认的是相对趋势和数量级判断，而不是完整表格数值。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读但要抓住方法骨架。先看 Introduction，把作者要反驳的两个直觉记住：一是“RoPE 既然相对，就不该有绝对位置”；二是“attention sink 应该是绝对位置信息的主要载体”。然后重点看 leakage metric 那一节，尤其是为什么必须用 saturated relative-offset baseline，而不能用简单线性模型；这是全文最容易被忽略、但决定结论是否可信的部分。接着看 bidirectional ablation 的定义和相关表格，理解作者如何把 causal mask 与 residual stream 分开。再看关于 position 0 closed dynamical system、sink-reading heads、BOS 移动/替换实验的部分，这里是机制解释最扎实的地方。阅读时建议把注意力分成三层：logit 层、softmax 层、residual-state 层。作者的贡献就在于说明绝对位置不是只在某一层“冒出来”，而是由这三层中的后两层共同引入。预计 30–45 分钟可以抓住主线；如果你在做长上下文、位置编码或 attention 机制分析，表 6 和相关消融值得细看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise mechanistic question: RoPE makes attention logits depend only on relative offsets, so why do trained decoder-only Transformers still encode absolute position in their attention patterns? The contribution is not merely to observe the leakage, but to localize its sources and trace the pathway by which absolute position enters attention. The main answer is that there are two structural sources. First, the causal mask makes the softmax denominator query-position dependent, so attention weights inherit absolute-position dependence even when logits are purely relative. Second, the residual stream develops position-varying states across layers, and later heads read those states back into queries and keys. The paper also argues against a common intuition: attention sinks are not rich content stores. Instead, the stronger mechanism comes from the closed dynamics at position 0 under causal attention, plus downstream sink-reading heads that propagate information originating there.","importance":"This matters for pretraining research because it separates a formal property of the positional encoding from the behavior of the trained network. RoPE may be relative at the logit level, yet the full architecture is not position-pure once masking, softmax normalization, BOS handling, and residual dynamics are taken into account. That distinction is directly relevant to long-context extrapolation, position generalization, RoPE variants, NTK scaling, and sliding-window attention. The paper also offers a usable decomposition of absolute-position leakage into a causal-mask component and a residual-stream component. That is valuable for mechanistic analysis and for architecture design if one wants a model that is closer to truly relative position processing. 1.55，但问答准确率反而下降 13 个点。\n\n第二部分是诊断工具 PRISM，由两个推理时分析组成。其一是 corruption test：在每一步生成时，对 latent 位置做三类干预——置零、加入高斯噪声、替换为别的样本的 latent——观察最终任务精度变化。如果 latent 真在承担推理，干预应显著伤害性能；但实验里所有变体的性能波动都不超过 4 个点，最差变体甚至在 latent 置零后更好。其二是双位置 linear probe：分别在线性探测 answer-decoding state 和 autoregressive feedback variable 上是否可解码出答案。结果是答案信息能从最终 answer-decoding hidden state 读出，却不能从被重新注入的 latent 变量中读出。作者据此提出一个信息瓶颈视角：训练目标中的主导项仍是 cross-entropy，它会把与答案相关的计算压力施加到任何能更直接服务输出的位置；而 cosine/MSE 这类重建损失既不约束表示压缩，也不保证表示保留与答案相关的信息，因此模型完全可能学会“绕过” latent，让辅助损失只作为一种参数整形信号存在。","experiments":"核心结果有四个。第一，五种 LVR visual reasoning: whether better alignment between supervised latent tokens and teacher visual targets actually means the model relies on those latents at inference, and therefore answers better. The answer is no. Using five controlled LVR variants trained with the same backbone, data, and step budget, the authors show that cosine/MSE-style alignment is not a faithful proxy for downstream performance; in fact, cosine is strongly negatively correlated with task accuracy. More importantly, inference-time interventions on the latent channel barely change performance, which suggests that the supervised latents are often not the load-bearing path for answer computation.","importance":"The value of the paper is diagnostic rather than architectural. It challenges a common interpretation of auxiliary supervision in multimodal models: that the representation being directly supervised is also the representation doing the work. The results suggest a different picture. Auxiliary losses may help mainly by reshaping shared parameters through gradient flow, while the supervised latent itself is bypassed at inference. This matters beyond LVR. The same failure mode can plausibly appear in intermediate-layer supervision, multimodal alignment objectives, latent chain-of-thought setups, and continuous reasoning channels in video or speech models. For pretraining research, the key lesson is to separate two questions: whether an auxiliary loss improves the model, and whether the supervised intermediate state is actually causally used.","technical_approach":"$4f","experiments":"$50","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, but it is worth reading with care if you work on intermediate supervision. analytically. Instead of modeling a full Transformer, it builds a high-dimensional attention model in which a large-rank component is pre-trained on abundant data and a rank-one LoRA component is later fitted on limited downstream data.","importance":"Its value is conceptual rather than immediately prescriptive. The paper provides a tractable pretrain-to-finetune theory for attention, with explicit predictions for test error and representation alignment. The most useful idea is that the effect of pre-training on LoRA can be summarized through an effective-noise mechanism. This is relevant for thinking about PEFT, continual pre-training, and downstream adaptation, but the conclusions should not be treated as direct hyperparameter rules for production LLMs.","technical_approach":"The model is a single-head tied-attention layer over sequences of length T and token dimension D. The pre-training stage learns a high-rank matrix W on an extensive-rank attention-indexed target, with L2 regularization. The fine-tuning stage freezes W and learns only a rank-one LoRA vector w from a smaller dataset. Keys and queries are tied, values are identity, and the activation is either row-wise softmax or a rescaled identity map. In the high-dimensional limit, with D, N, N', and the rank P scaling together, both stages are characterized by a finite set of scalar order parameters. These parameters yield sharp predictions for pre-training error, fine-tuning error, and overlaps with the ground-truth structure. Compared with NTK-style LoRA analyses, the emphasis is on feature learning after pre-training rather than lazy dynamics.","experiments":"The main results are theoretical learning-curve characterizations, not empirical LLM evaluations. The paper identifies an effective-noise quantity that mediates the impact of pre-training quality on LoRA performance and uses it to derive prescriptions for pre-training and idealized fine-tuning data selection. It also describes regimes where low fine-tuning test error does not imply good reconstruction overlap, due to memorization effects from the pre-training data. Based on the provided content, there are no real-model LoRA numbers or large-scale language-model experiments to report.","reading_guide":"Plan for a 30-minute skim. Read the two-stage model carefully first: the definitions of W, w, the sample sizes, the attention activation, and the regularized objectives determine what the theory does and does not cover. Then inspect the main asymptotic characterization and the order-parameter equations. The effective-noise section is the most useful part for LLM researchers, because it connects pre-training quality to adaptation performance.
